## Chapter 32
### Concerning the Seven Works of the Living Spirit

*This chapter is largely a compact, systematic enumeration of the Living Spirit’s cosmic “seven works” (plus an additional end-time work), in an impersonal cosmological register. The main later material is the dialogue/frame attribution at the opening and a concluding beatitude-style blessing that reads like an editorial devotional tag rather than the older expository substrate.*

The Living Spirit has done seven [wo]rks with his strength.

[The fi]rst: He brought the First Man up from the contest, the way a pearl is [brought] up from the sea.

[The second: He spread about the one who had rebelled, he crucified [h]im in every body.

The third: He [tr]ampled, piled up, and pierced the essences of death.

[The f]ourth: He constructed the ships of light.

The fi[f]th: He evoked his five sons, and distributed them about. They [took] possession of the zone, they bore up under all the universe's weight. Also, he evoked three living words. He set [them] upon the th[re]e vessels, another one upon the giant. [Again, he evoked the] summons, that it might be mixed with the five shining men.

The sixth: When he had constructed the zone he aro[se] and s[e]nt forth a multitude of powers and many [angel]s; so that they would bound the circuit of the z[o]ne on every si[de], un[t]il he finished [co]nstructing the works. And at the end [of] all the [wor]ks he to[ok s]ome of them into his storehouses, and he appointed others at the watch-posts.

The [sev]enth: A[t the] time when the Ambass[ador dis]pl[aye]d his glorious imag[e], the L[iving] Spirit [established] many [go]ds and angels. They [ ... ] Th[ey to]ok hold of it, that the entire building [ ... ] not [ ... / ... ] one another against [the] light, which he had [ ... ]

Also another gr[ea]t and glorious work he will enact at the e[nd] is [the] Last [Statue], which he will bring up to the aeons of [light]; and he [ ... ] in, and [he ta]kes hold, and he smites [ ... ], and [he] builds, and he [ ... ], and he [ ... t]hem.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Initial frame formula (“Once again… speaks”) marks compilation layer; the remaining clause is the chapter’s structural incipit for the older numbered teaching sequence.
- ¶2: Numbered work-list in cosmic terms (Living Spirit → First Man rescue) with internal correspondential simile (pearl/sea) and no institutional/application pivot.
- ¶3: Continues the impersonal enumerative sequence; “crucified…in every body” functions as cosmological process-description rather than NT citation or church application.
- ¶4: Cosmic combat/process verbs in a numbered schema; no frame, no exhortation, no institutional referents.
- ¶5: Brief cosmological building-act within the fixed seven-works structure (cosmos→cosmos).
- ¶6: High-value substrate markers: structured numeration; five/three groupings; “zone,” “vessels,” “giant,” “summons” as internal cosmological mechanics without externalizing to contemporary institutions.
- ¶7: Administrative-cosmic ordering (storehouses, watch-posts) remains within the universe’s architecture; no ecclesial offices or paraenesis.
- ¶8: Despite lacunae, the seventh-work slot is clearly cosmological (Ambassador’s image; establishment of gods/angels; stabilizing the ‘building’ against Light).
- ¶9: Eschatological-cosmic action (Last Statue; aeons of Light) remains internal to the Manichaean redemption mechanics, not NT citation or church regulation.
- ¶10: Beatitude-style blessing (“Blessed is he…”) reads as a devotional/editorial closure rather than the impersonal mechanical exposition typical of the oldest numbered teaching substrate.

## Chapter 33
### Concerning the Five Things that he constructed with the hard Bodies of the Rulers

*A very short, largely intact cosmological list-teaching: five cosmic constructions made by the Living Spirit from the hard bodies of the Rulers. The only clear late element is the formulaic dialogue/attribution opener in ¶1.*

The Living Spirit constructed five great things from veins and skull[s in the] hard [bodies].

The first is the disc that is placed upon [the sh]oulder of the Porter.

The se[con]d are the arches of [the] pillar that is before him.

[The th]ird is the [mou]ld of the fo[r]m for the gehennas.

The fourth are the [seven (?)] pillars that he has erected in the great sea, in [the] seven parts of the universe.

The fifth are the four walls that surround the vessels.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Core cosmological exposition (Living Spirit as cosmic artisan; construction-from-demon-bodies motif) preceded by a formulaic speaker-attribution, which marks later editorial framing.
- ¶2: Systematic numbered enumeration of cosmic architecture (item 1 of 5), staying entirely within the Manichaean cosmos (Porter as a cosmological being).
- ¶3: Continues the same impersonal, structural fivefold list; purely cosmic referents (pillar/arches) with no application to contemporary institutions.
- ¶4: Cosmic-mechanical itemization (a ‘mould/form’ for cosmic prisons), with lacunae preserved; no citations, exhortation, or institutional mapping.
- ¶5: Correspondential-cosmological structuring (seven pillars; seven parts of the universe) in an expository register typical of the older teaching substrate.
- ¶6: Completes the fivefold construction list; still ‘cosmos→cosmos’ description (walls/vessels as cosmic structures) without editorial application voice.

## Chapter 34
### Concerning the Ten Things that the Ambassador began by his Advent

*This chapter is almost entirely a compact, numbered cosmological sequence describing ten system-events initiated by the Third Ambassador’s advent (ships, wheels, separation of Night/Day, fall of Sin/abortions, transmigrations). Apart from a brief formulaic opener, there is no exhortation, institutional application, or NT overlay; several items are lacunose.*

The first: The Great Builder came forth to build the new aeon.

The second: The ships moved through the heights in the heavens.

The third: The wheels begin to send the life to earth and the upper worlds.

The fourth: [ ... ]

The fifth is the doors of the ships, that they were opened up [ ...

The sixth is the doors too [ ... ... that they were opened to receive the waste.

The seventh is [the Sin], which he had separated from the rulers by cause of the Third Ambassador; it fell upon the earth.

The eighth: Night and day were separated [ ... ... ]

The ninth: The abortions [fell] to the ground.

The tenth: Transmigrations came about in the zone.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Formulaic compilation frame: “Once again the enlightener speaks …”, presenting the teaching as a reported discourse.
- ¶2: Numbered cosmological list-item in impersonal expository voice; no citation or institutional application.
- ¶3: Systematic cosmological mechanism (ships/heavens) presented as part of an ordered decade of events.
- ¶4: Cosmic-machine register (“wheels” transmitting “life”) consistent with the old mechanistic substrate style.
- ¶5: Still part of the numbered core sequence; content lost to lacuna.
- ¶6: Cosmological process-description (doors/ships opened) within the tenfold scheme; lacunose continuation.
- ¶7: Continues the same cosmic-mechanism list; “waste” fits purification-machine discourse; lacunae present.
- ¶8: Internal cosmic action (separation from rulers; descent to earth) with personified agent (“Sin” as a cosmological entity), not moral exhortation or contemporary polemic.
- ¶9: Cosmic structuring of time/day-night as an event in the decade; lacunose but clearly cosmological.
- ¶10: Brief but still a cosmological event item (mythic/cosmic fallout) in the enumerated structure.
- ¶11: Cosmic process (“transmigrations” in the zone) closing the tenfold mechanism-list; impersonal, non-institutional voice.

## Chapter 35
### Concerning the Four Works of the Ambassador

*This chapter is dominated by a compact, systematic fourfold enumeration of cosmological effects of the Ambassador’s self-manifestation (image, firmament-doors, purification/releasing of Light, appearance of the Light Ship/Living Soul). The only clear later material is the hagiographic/dialogue-style attribution in ¶1, which frames an otherwise core teaching sentence.*

At the time when the Ambassador displayed his image, four great works were revealed that are very excellent and outstanding.

The first is his image that he displayed in a glorious image.

The second is all the doors of the firmaments that were opened at that time. They open [...] and shut behind him.

The third is the Light and the releasing that was purified by his image. That Light is more than the light that he shall [...]

The fourth: He has [made apparent], by the perfection of people, his Light Ship. He has [...] the Living Soul that shone forth in perfection and fullness over the worlds of enmity.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: The clause is a neutral, impersonal set-up for a numbered teaching list (core-style), but it is introduced by a formulaic speaker-attribution typical of the later hagiographic frame; hence MIXED with the frame stripped.
- ¶2: Purely internal cosmological enumeration (“The first…”) describing what the Ambassador’s work consists in; no citation, audience address, or institutional application.
- ¶3: Systematic second item in the fourfold series; wholly within cosmic architecture (firmaments/doors), with no move into contemporary institutions or exhortation.
- ¶4: Cosmological process-language (purification/releasing of Light) continuing the numbered structure; the comparison remains within cosmological description despite minor register noise in pastoral scoring.
- ¶5: Closes the fourfold core list with internal cosmic entities/vehicles (Light Ship, Living Soul) and a salvation-mechanics register; “perfection of people” functions here as an impersonal soteriological condition rather than an institutional exhortation.

## Chapter 36
### Concerning the Wheel that exists in front of the King of Honour

*This chapter is overwhelmingly systematic cosmological exposition: the Wheel before the King of Honour, its seals, and its function for binding/diagnosing the rulers and revealing the five shining men through the zone. The only clear later material is the dialogue-attribution frame (“Again the apostle speaks…”) at the start of ¶1, which is removed in extraction. No NT citation or institutional/pastoral application appears in the surviving text.*

about the wheel that exists in front [of the King of Honour who dwells in] the seventh firmament [...], the root of the rulers above and below in a great and cruel fetter; as the entire will of the great King of Honour is placed in [it]. Also, their entire enmity, which is in the firmaments, is [...] and ensnared in it.

It exists in a blaze of splendour [with] ornamentation and great beauty. Now, there are twelve seals in it. The seal between i[s] every firmament and the rulers who are there [... ...] are in it; for should one of them wish to escape, he will be bound [...]. If he should wish to cause a trick in his bond [... will be] apparent and known in [that] wheel [...]. The spheres and stars and [the] leader of all the powers exist there, and can be known by it, in front of the King of Honour.

The twelf[t]h seal that is in it is its five shining men that are conjoined and enmeshed through the entire zone; because every place where they are cramped, and every place where they have space, is displayed by that wheel. For the root of all the firmaments above and below, and that of their rulers, is ensnared and fixed in it.

The observation [...] is set right at all times opposite the great King o[f Honour ...] so that fear, wh[ich ...] will assume all the powers of mixture [... ...] their root affixed there, so that they cannot make an esc[a]pe from the fetter of their bond.

That wheel rese[m]bles, in this respect, a chain in front of the chief guard, for when [...] in th[ose ...] in the [... cha]in.

This is also the case for all the rulers who are in the firmaments, and every power of the zone: If they should wish to escape, they shall be recognised and revealed by that wheel. [As] the wheel is like a great mirror, for [the di]scrimination of all things [... is] in it.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Core cosmological mechanism (Wheel as binding/ensnaring device in the 7th firmament), preceded by a clear hagiographic compilation cue (“apostle speaks”), hence MIXED.
- ¶2: Systematic description (twelve seals; firmaments/rulers; escape/trick detection) stays entirely within the cosmic architecture; impersonal expository voice.
- ¶3: Cosmos→cosmos mapping: the “five shining men” (soul dispersed through the zone) are revealed/located by the Wheel; no application to contemporary institutions.
- ¶4: Functional cosmic surveillance/binding language (“observation… opposite… cannot make an escape”) with no shift to exhortation or church practice; lacunae limit detail but register remains cosmological.
- ¶5: Correspondential simile (Wheel ~ chain/guard) continues the mechanism-description. Fragmentary, but still internal analogy rather than institutional pointing.
- ¶6: Ends with a classic substrate-style correspondence (“wheel… like a great mirror”) describing cosmic discrimination/revelation, with no frame, citation, or pastoral address.

## Chapter 37
### Concerning the Three Zones.

*The chapter is overwhelmingly a systematic cosmological teaching on the three-zone construction of the universe (Darkness powers → sons of the Man → sons of the Living Spirit), with only a brief hagiographic/dialogue-style incipit. No NT citations or pastoral/application pivots appear; the mappings remain cosmos→cosmos throughout. The final paragraph is fragmentary (lacunae) but still within the same structural exposition (King of Honour in the firmaments/universe).*

When he built and constructed the worlds, he suspended the zones on their zones, and within another zone also. The first zone is the five powers of Darkness, males and females.

This is why he hung one zone on a zone! He hung the five powers of Darkness on the five shining men, the sons of the Man; because all the enmity is entwined and bound in them.

Also, within the zone are the five sons of the [Living] Spirit; because the entire edifice is suspended from them, it being [ ... ... he]ard (?) him too, the oth[ers ... ... ] after them.

And this is the one who came to [ ... ... the fi]rmaments above [ ... ] which is in one zone, is the great [King of Honour] who e[xists ... ] within the [ ... ... ] of the universe.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Formulaic opening attribution (“Once again the enlightener speaks…”) is a classic Kephalaia hagiographic frame marker.
- ¶2: Impersonal cosmological mechanics (“built…constructed…suspended”) and a numbered structural identification of zones; no institutional or exhortative application.
- ¶3: Continues the internal cosmic architecture via a direct mapping (Darkness powers → shining men/sons of the Man) with functional rationale; stays within the system.
- ¶4: Systematic five-fold cosmological structuring (sons of the Living Spirit) and architectural dependence (“edifice…suspended”)—a substrate-style ‘how it works’ description; lacunae reflect manuscript damage, not layering.
- ¶5: Despite heavy lacunae, it names a key cosmic being (King of Honour) and locates him in the firmaments/universe—still cosmological exposition without frame/pastoral/NT overlay.

## Chapter 38
### Concerning the Light Mind and the Apostles and the Saints

*The chapter’s oldest substrate dominates the middle: a systematic macrocosm↔microcosm correspondence (universe-as-human; five/six-fold sons; watch-district rebellions; then the body’s five-member binding and the Light Mind’s reversal). The opening Q&A and the closing apostolate-boast are hagiographic frame; the second-half drift (¶44–50) is clearly institutional/pastoral (church officers, rebuke, community management). Several paragraphs are MIXED where the editor appends institutional application (church/apostles persecution; idolatry polemic) to otherwise cosmological teaching.*

All the error, when the en[e]my of the lights constructed it, he constructed after the likeness of a man. The head of the universe is the beginning of the garments. [H]is neck is the nape of the garm[ents]. His stomach is the five unfolded ones (?), which a[re ... ] of the garm[e]nts. His ribs are all the firmament[s]. His navel is the sphere of the stars and the signs of the zodiac. And, also, [the par]ts [that] come from his navel to his hip are [...] that come from the sphere t[o the corne]rs of the four worlds. His loins are the th[ree eart]hs that are below [...] upon the head of the Porter. His [...] from the [...] to the earth upon which the Porter [is stood] firm. His shins and his feet a[re ...] and the entire zone that belongs [...]

His heart is mankind. His liver is the four-footed animals. His lung is the race of birds that [fly in] the air. His spleen is the race of fish that swim in the waters. His kidneys are the world of reptiles that creep [up]on the earth. His outer skin is the wall that [... which] surrounds the piercing and the great fire. His [...] the vessels of the great fire. His [...] of darkness. His gall is the [... His] great intestine is the breadth of the great [...] of the worlds. His veins [...] all [the spri]ngs and wells. His e[yes (?) ...] His feet are his [...]

This is how each of the worlds h[ave been ha]rmonised (?). Five gods are fastened in him. [...] they are his soul and his life [...] man is a sinner [...] the Living Spirit and the Ambassador [...] the five sleepless guardians.

He has [...] the Keeper of Splendour, the mind that is [...] in the wo[r]ld of the mind that is above. He has [...] who]le [...] of the powers of hea[ven that] are in [...] of the great King of Honour, [who is the thought that exists i]n the seventh firmament. He has humbled [...] also of the Adamant of Lig[h]t, who is the insight [... ] from he has given [...] because of lust [... He has] also appointed the King of Glory, who is the coun[sel ...] patience over the three images; that of wind, that of fire, and that of water. He has handed over to him the [...] do evilness. [...] the Porter, who is the consideration [...] the wi]sdom in the [...] which is below. He has made him [...] the foundation [below ...]

Furthermore: The Ambassador refined from them five intellectuals of life. Also, the summons and the obedience were situated there. Now, they made six sons of the Living Spirit, together with the six sons of the First Man!

Further: The Ambassador placed in them the great Mind; who is the Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man.

Furthermore: Jesus the Youth was set there; who is the image of the living word, of the utterance and the obedience.

He has made strong these camps, those above and those below [...], (so that) each one of them will be secure in the circuit of his watch-district, so that neither uprising nor treachery are made in his watch-district.

And look, see! The Keeper of Splendour is set firm in the great mind, in the camp above the prison of the bound ones, for he brings to nothing all the gloom of death. An[d a] treachery came about, and an uprising! The Sin abor[te]d, [it t]angled i]n with the soul. It became mixed with this light that it expelled toward the image of the Ambassador. It went [...] in the third firmament that is above the watch-t[ower ...] the Keeper of Splendour. From that place also it tangled in with the light. It was detached and came down to that which is dry and that which is moist. It [fashio]ned the trees [up]on the dry (land); but in the sea it immediat[ely] took form and made a great uprising in the sea.

Again, look, see! The great King of Honour, who is the thought, he is in the thi[r]d firmament. He is made [...] with wrath. And an uprising came about! A treachery and a[n] anger happened in his camp. The watchers of heaven, who came down to the earth in his watch-district, they did all the deeds of treachery. They have revealed crafts in the world and have unveiled to people the mysteries of heaven. An uprising came about, and a destruction, on the earth [... ] to it.

The Adamant [...] the fulfillment. He is set firm upo[n ...] and a treachery came about in his camp; the occasion when the abortions fell to the earth. They formed Adam and Eve. They begat them so as to reign through them in the world. They fashioned every object of lust upon the earth. The entire world was filled by their lust.

Once again: In the watch of the great King of Glory, wh[o] is the great counsel, he who exercises authority over the thre[e] wheels. A [dis]turbance came about, and an affliction, for [they were ... ] pained and oppressed in the three earths. After the Ambassador displayed his image, the paths closed to their [...] their ascent was impeded by them [...] the w[ind], the water, and the fire ascend on them.

[O]nce again: In the watch of the Porter, he who humiliates [...] upr]ising of the abysses below [...] bent, the fastenings underneath were loosened [...] in the foundation below.

On account of the earthquake that happened in the watch of the Keeper of Splen[dour], the Pillar of Glory came forth; as it [...] helper of the Keeper of Splendour. It bore up under all [burden]s.

Conversely, because of the treachery and the uprising that happened in the watch of the great King of Honour, which is the watchers who came down to earth from the heavens; four angels were called upon about them. They bound the watche[r]s with an eternal chain, in the prison of the blackened ones (?). [Th]ey obliterated their children from upon the earth.

[Th]en, again, the abortions descended in the watch of the Adamant and begat Adam and Eve; because of that great treachery wh[ich] happened, and the mystery of wickedness, he sent Jesus [...] the prayer of the five sons. He assumed them [...] the abortions. He fastened them beneath the [...] the mind of Adam.

Also, because of the earthquake that happened in these three earths, and in that the paths were hindered and the springs of wind and water and fire were impeded, Jesus cast himself down. He assumed Eve; and he straightened the tracks of the [win]d, the water, and the fire. He opened the springs for them, and he set [in ord]er the path of their ascent.

Once [again]: Since the earth beneath the Porter was loose[ned from] the fastenings that [...] because of this too Jesus went down, assuming [Ev]e until he reached tha[t] place. [H]e set in [or]der and stre[ng]th[ened] the fastenings beneath. He returned, [he came] up to his [...] rest.

Look at all these watches of the zon[e], the ones in which these great gods are master, w[atch]ing over them [...]. Uprisings have happened, and treachery, in them. The [...] in them from time to time; with a great hum[iliati]on, until they humbled the powers of the enmity.

So also is this body! A mighty power lives here, even if it is small in its stature. Nevertheless, Sin dwells within, and the old man who is lodged in it. Certainly he is cruel, with great cunning; until the Lig/ht Mind finds how to humble this body, and drive it [according to] his pleasure.

Just so, in the w[atch-]districts of his great outer brothers, they who are masters in the zone: in them, in the great body, the earthquake and treachery happened from time to time. So, also, is the watch-district of the Mind, which is the body of the flesh. Sin raises [up], from time to time, its agitations in the body.

Now you, understand thus, that the powers of light are good. The beginning and the end are unveiled to them. All that they do is being done with correct judgement. Indeed, because of this they may permit the enmity to initiate error, and do its pleasure for a moment. Then they seize [...] they have acted first with a righteous judgement.

And [with resp]ect to this other (question) that you have put forward: How may the Light Mind come, this great and honoured mighty one, and assume this little body of the flesh? Again, look, see! These gods, in that [...] are great and mighty ones. And each one of them is enclosed and hard pressed in this place wherein he is set; like trees holding to their taproot. So also this is how each one of them has held to his taproot in the world, according to the k[ind] of place in which he is set, bearing up till [...]

Now, also, happen you know [...] the world is set firm, being ordered [...] the five sons of the Living Spirit in all its members [...]. Sin took this body out from [the] la[nd (?) ... co]nstructed it in its members. It took its [bod]y from the five b]odies of darkness. (Sin) constructed the body. Yet, its [sou]l i[t] took from the five shining gods.

(Sin) bou]nd (the soul) in the five members of the body. It bound the mind in bone; the thought in sinew; the insight in vein; the counsel in flesh; the consideration in ski[n].

(Sin) set f[ast] its five powers: its mind upon the mind of the soul; its thought upon the thought of the soul; its insight upon the insight of the soul; its counsel upon the counsel of the soul; its consider[atio]n upon the consideration of the soul. It placed its five [an]gels and authorities upon the five members of the soul, which it had brought in and bound in the flesh. They [...], pronouncing to the soul and continually leading it on to every evil thi[n]g,

(The soul) assumed error and forgetfulness. It for[go]t its essence and its race and its kindred; kn[ow]ing not the door of the place to pray to him, nor call up to him. It became hostile to its father [...] itself, being wicked [...] its own light [...]

The Light Mind comes and finds the soul [...] it assuming it in the [...] its wisdom [...] he shall become for it [...] the bonds [...] members in the body. He shall loosen the m[ind of the soul and relea]se it from the bone. He shall release the th[ought of the soul] from the sinew; and s[o] bind the thought [of the Sin in] the sinew. He shall release the insight of the soul from the vei[n]; and so bind the insight of the Sin in the vein. [He sh]all loosen the counsel of the soul, and release it from the flesh; and so bind the counsel of the Sin in the flesh. He shall release the consideration of the soul from the skin; and bin[d] the consideration of the Sin in the skin.

This is how he shall release the members of the soul, and make them free from the five members of Sin. Conversely, these five members of Sin, which were loose, he shall bind them.

He shall set right the members of the soul; form and purify them, and construct a new man of them, a child of righteousness. [And] when [he] fashions and const[r]ucts and purifies the new man; then he shall bring forth five great living members out from the five great memb[ers]. And he places them in the members of the new man. He shall place his m[ind], which is love, in the mind of the new man. Also, the th[oug]ht, which is faith, he shall pl[ace in] the thought of the new man; whom he shall purify. His insight, which [is perfection, he shall place] in the insight of the new man. His counsel, which [is] patience, he shall place in his counsel. Also, [w]isdom, which is his consideration, in the consideration of the new m[an]. He shall make the image of the word pure from the word of Sin; and he adds to it his word, so that his [...] becomes nourisher and strengthener [...]

Now, when he shall perf[ect ... the tw]elve members. This [is how ...] and his wisdom. His [...] becomes righteous, as he perf[ects ...] While formerly he was running [...] but instead of this, now he runs [...] his road and his path and his [...] also [he mounts up] to the heights, to the great aeons [...] thus the old man is bound in the [...] and his lust, his [...] his foolishness in these five members of the bo[dy ...] the dark spirit is imprisoned with them in a bond and in severe misery.

Also, the new man reigns by his love, by hi[s] faith, by his perfection, by his patience, and by his wisdom. Yet, his king [...] the Light Mind, he w[ho is] king over the totality. He re[igns] o[ver it according to] his pleasure. While the members [...], thus Sin is imprisoned. Still, the Light Mind is king, and an affliction may arise in the body from t[im]e to time.

There are occasions when Sin shall mount up with its foolishness; and it disturbs the consideration, and it clouds the wisdom and the understanding of the person. It causes truth to be split into doubt in him, and he utters some foo[li]sh words and some [...]

[T]hen, have I taught you, and opened your (sg.) [eyes], as to how confusion shall arise in the zo[ne], in the camps of these great mig[hty] gods? Again, this is how disturbance shall come about, from ti[me] to time, in the [...] of the [Li]ght [M]ind.

So al[so] these watch-keepers, for they are set [...] fi[rm in] the zone, (but) they are not visible. Again, this too is the way of the Light Mind, [for he is] not visible in the body.

Also, [corr]esponding to th[ese] outer [watch-keepers]: in that while they are great, [y]et have they contorted themselves. They have [...], they have become small, to the measure of the task that is apportioned to each o[n]e [of t]hem. Again, this too is like the Mind, himse[lf] a great one, and exalted; but he is bent over and [has] become small, to this little worthless [bo]dy.

[Even so] the gods in the outer [z]one: they are transcendent and purified, they are set [f]a[st in] the mingling of the totality, (but) are not defiled. [This too is like the] Light [Mind]. While he is placed in hi[s ...]

Again, behold the mightiness and the activity of the Light Mind: how vast he is over all the watch-districts of the body! He stays fast at his camp. He shuts off all deliberations of the body from the beguilements of Sin. He limits them and distributes them out. He sets them down at his pleasure.

Also, he does another work, surpassing and ex/ceeding (these). He bestows a great spirit upon the ele/ct one. Indeed, now may you find him, as he stands on the earth, [r]ising up in his heart and ascending to the Father, the G[o]d of truth. He who exists and is established above all the things of loss. [Onc]e again, he may push dow[n] in his insight and his consideration, and descend to the land of Darkness, from where the Darkness has poured forth. His heart shall run and touch everything, as it [...]

I have taught you] the deeds of the [Light] Mind! Whoever has an open and percept[ive] eye, [he can] appear to him. Whoever has not that eye, he can not appear to him.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Hagiographic dialogue container (“Once again… a disciple… the [apostle]”), reporting prior teaching rather than expounding it directly; Q&A frame signals compilation layer.
- ¶2: Continuation of the disciple’s question (“I see… rebellions arise…”); interrogative frame rather than impersonal exposition.
- ¶3: Second interrogative (“I ask you…”), still within Q&A container; no systematic exposition.
- ¶4: Fragmentary third question; question-voice belongs to the compilation frame.
- ¶5: Fourth question (“why is his likeness not displayed…”), Q&A frame.
- ¶6: Fifth question about apostolate and persecution “in the world” (hagiographic topic), still dialogue frame.
- ¶7: Entreaty formula (“I entreat you…”): explicit frame marker.
- ¶8: Dialogue attribution + institutional anchoring (“my chu[r]c[h]”); editorial/hagiographic apparatus.
- ¶9: Dialogue continuation (“disciple says… [apost]l[e]”); frame.
- ¶10: Dialogue attribution (“Then the apostle says…”) + performative rhetoric (“give vision…”); not impersonal system-description.
- ¶11: Core correspondential substrate: universe-as-human body map (domain→domain), impersonal systematic anatomy-to-cosmos correspondences. Mixed only because of the dialogue attribution.
- ¶12: Systematic macrocosm↔microcosm mapping in correspondential registers; no institutional anchoring.
- ¶13: Cosmic-system integration (five gods/guardians) continuing the body-universe harmonisation; minor moral wording does not pivot to institutions.
- ¶14: Five-fold intellectual qualities mapped onto cosmic watch-keepers/firmaments; impersonal structural exposition (core).
- ¶15: Numbered/structured cosmology (five→six sons; beings-as-functions) is characteristic of the teaching substrate.
- ¶16: Systematic identification within the cosmic hierarchy (Mind=Pillar of Glory=Perfect Man).
- ¶17: ‘Jesus the Youth’ functions here as a defined cosmic hypostasis (not a Gospel citation), embedded in the six-son structure.
- ¶18: Cosmic ‘watch-district’ governance mechanics; impersonal, systemic voice.
- ¶19: Cosmic rebellion narrative remains internal to the system (Sin as a cosmological agent affecting firmaments/elements).
- ¶20: Mythic cosmogonic history tied to a specific watch-district; still cosmos→cosmos (watchers/firmament/earth as arena), not church-specific administration.
- ¶21: Core cosmogonic account (abortions→Adam/Eve→lust-filling world) is old; the appended persecution of ‘churches/apostles’ anchors to institutional present.
- ¶22: System mechanics (three wheels/three earths; ascent-path impediments; element ascent) remain within the cosmological architecture.
- ¶23: Cosmic foundation/fastenings/abysses—structural cosmology language.
- ¶24: Internal cosmic causality: watch-district event → emanation/response by a named cosmic entity.
- ¶25: Cosmic juridical mechanism (binding/chain/prison) described as process, not moral exhortation.
- ¶26: Mythic intervention by ‘Jesus’ as a cosmic agent (no Gospel citation formula), tied to five-sons structure.
- ¶27: Elemental ascent mechanics and cosmic descent/ascent action; remains within system description.
- ¶28: Cosmic engineering of ‘fastenings’ beneath the Porter; mythic process narrative.
- ¶29: Summative cosmological inference (watch-district pattern) is core; the only late element is the dialogue tag.
- ¶30: Macrocosm→microcosm analogy stated as system principle (body as watch-district; Sin/old man as internal rebels).
- ¶31: Explicit correspondence: outer watch-districts ↔ body of flesh; internal-to-system mapping.
- ¶32: Theodicy/providence logic stated cosmologically (powers of light vs enmity) without institutional application.
- ¶33: Impersonal explanatory analogy (enclosed gods in their stations) applied to Light Mind’s embodiment; still system-description.
- ¶34: Despite the algorithmic seam flag, the paragraph stays cosmos→cosmos (five sons; five bodies of darkness; five shining gods) and continues the five-fold microcosmic construction logic.
- ¶35: Canonical five-fold correspondence (intellectual faculties ↔ body tissues) is substrate-like, purely structural.
- ¶36: The five-on-five overlay mechanism is core; the appended anti-idolatry polemic is a later application layer.
- ¶37: Cosmic-psychological consequence (forgetting essence/kindred) flows from the five-fold binding; still not institutional.
- ¶38: Perfectly substrate-like reversal operation: release/bind across corresponding members; impersonal mechanics.
- ¶39: Five-fold system restated as lawlike mechanism (freeing/binding), fully internal to the cosmology.
- ¶40: Systematic five-to-five transformation: virtues correspond to liberated faculties; still presented as structural process, not church discipline.
- ¶41: Continuation of transformation mechanics (twelve-members completion; ascent); still not anchored to institutions.
- ¶42: Five-virtue reign + higher kingship of Light Mind: degree-structured anthropology (core).
- ¶43: Relapse mechanics described as internal disturbance of faculties; no institutional ‘fix’ yet, so still core psychological-cosmic teaching.
- ¶44: Institutional response sequence: “in the church, the teachers… presbyters shall gather…”—community governance, not cosmic correspondence.
- ¶45: Exhortational/community-management register (“rebuke… edification of his brethren…”) with application voice; editorial pastoral drift.
- ¶46: Behavioral/institutional diagnostics (separating from teacher/brethren; solitary habits).
- ¶47: Church-based intervention (“wise ones of the church shall gather…”) and moral exhortation; institutional layer.
- ¶48: Concluding moral assurance about victory over Sin, embedded in the pastoral disciplinary sequence.
- ¶49: Application to relations with “teacher… instructor… love of the church”; institutional anchoring (cosmos→community life).
- ¶50: Explicit separation from church and return to ‘worldly men’; community boundary-policing typical of later pastoral layer.
- ¶51: Returns to macrocosm patterning (zone/camps) and correlates it to Light Mind disturbances—systemic correspondence voice.
- ¶52: Explicit correspondence (invisible watch-keepers ↔ invisible Light Mind) is core.
- ¶53: Degree/condensation teaching (great→small by office) presented as lawlike correspondence; core style.
- ¶54: Purity-within-mingling principle stated cosmologically; correspondence applied to Light Mind in body.
- ¶55: Internal governance mechanics of faculties (‘deliberations… limits… distributes’) in correspondence with watch-district control.
- ¶56: Mystical anthropology expressed as macrocosmic mobility of faculties (ascend/descend) still within the cosmological system; ‘elect’ may reflect later communal terminology, but the operation remains system-internal rather than institutional regulation.
- ¶57: Aphoristic visibility doctrine (perceptive eye) fits teaching voice; mixed only due to the dialogue tag.
- ¶58: Transition into self-referential ‘my work… my leadership’—hagiographic/apostolic frame, not core cosmology.
- ¶59: Autobiographical boast (“I, a single Manichaios…”) and contemporary world/wealth imagery; hagiographic layer.
- ¶60: Victorious-apostle rhetoric over cities/countries; biographical apologetic typical of compilation/community frame.
- ¶61: Application voice + political powers (“kings… nobles… officials”); hagiographic defense of Mani’s authority.
- ¶62: Self-praise of liberating ‘children/brothers/kin’; community/leadership rhetoric rather than system mapping.
- ¶63: Protective ‘breastplate of wisdom’ for ‘my children’; institutional leader voice, not impersonal cosmology.
- ¶64: Explicit church-building and offices (“elect[ion]… apostle… of the church… my church… apostles and ambassa[d]ors I have sent”): hagiographic/institutional layer.
- ¶65: Comparative apostolic boasting (“former apo[st]les… apart from Jesus only”); community legitimation frame.
- ¶66: Grand hagiographic closure claim (“door… opened by me… to the gods and the angels and mankind”); not core exposition.
- ¶67: Formal closing response/praise and promise of obedience (“commandments… faith”); editorial frame conclusion.

## Chapter 39
### Concerning the Three Days and the Two Deaths

*Despite a formulaic dialogue opening, this chapter is dominated by systematic cosmological-esoteric periodization (three “days” / two “deaths”) expressed in impersonal, structural terms (First Man descent, purification cycle, new aeon, unveiling, separation in fire). The only clear late material is the dialogue frame (¶1–3) and a moralizing/NT-colored tail in the “second death” description (¶15).*

that there exist three great mighty days in all; and also two great and bitter deaths.

The first day is our father, the Man. From the time when the First Man went down to the abyss of darkness and drowned in it; and the Living Spirit was sent, who went and bore him up. After the Man had ascended, the Father of Life and the Great Spirit set things in order, and the worlds above and below.

Furthermore, the glorious Ambassador came and dwelt in the ship of the day. He displayed his glorious image, and he purified the light out of all things. He walked in the heights of the heavens. Till the time when the world will be dissolved, all things destroyed, the great fire released, and the Last Statue ascends. These three deeds were begun on this first day, when they sent the First Man with his five sons.

In contrast, [the first death] is the Darkness that mixed with the five sons of the First Man.

The second day is the time when the fathers of light, who were victorious in the struggle, will sit on their thrones in the new aeon and dwell on the new earth. And they reign in the new aeon till the Father unveils his image above them.

The length of the sojourn in the new aeon equals the measure of the first day spent in affliction. They will reign like this and rejoice in the new aeon.

The great third day [is the time] when the Father will unveil over them his image ... ... and he raises them up [ ... ] and he receives them into his hidden treasury ... and he gives [ ... ] his soul. He can pour out upon them ... his ambrosia and his sweet perfume, which takes away all affliction that they have seen with their eyes. [ ... ] fullness and a joy eternal! When his grace would sate them, he gives to them his [ ... ] and they are made anew in them.

Also, he can summon them once more in peace and silence, and perfect their stature in same measure as his first aeons. And he sets each power in its aeon, in its dwelling-place. They will dwell in the new aeon in their aeons!

Now, when they dwell in their aeons, they will become rich beyond measure, for ever, beyond time. From then on they will not count that season amongst them; nor the number of the days, nor the hours, generation after generation, for ever and ever. The Father will not be hidden from them, from this time on. Rather, he has accomplished (his) unveiling to them [ ... ] he being revealed to them for ever.

These are the three great days, [which the Father of Greatness has given number to in the reckoning].

The first death is from the time when the light fell to the Darkness, and was mixed in with the rulers of darkness; until the time when the light will become pure, and be separated from the darkness in that great fire. The remainder left behind there can build and add to that Last Statue.

Now, the second death is the death in which the souls of sinful men shall die; when [they will] be stripped of the shining light that illuminates the world. And also they are separated from the living air, from which they receive [living] breath; and they are deprived of this living soul, which [ ... ] in the world; and they are separated and swept away from this Last Statue [ ... ... ] and bound on [ ... ... ] the female will become [ ... ] their torture and their affliction;

These are the two deaths. The first death is temporal; [but] the second death is eternal. It is the sec[ond deat]h!

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Opens with explicit dialogue attribution (“apostle…disciples”) and direct address (“my beloved ones”), but the numbered schematic (three days / two deaths) is the underlying teaching-structure cue likely older than the frame.
- ¶2: Q&A frame: disciples addressing “our father” and requesting explanation; functions as editorial container rather than substrate exposition.
- ¶3: Pure dialogue linkage (“He speaks to them.”) with no teaching content.
- ¶4: Impersonal cosmological narrative used structurally to define a temporal period; entirely internal to the Manichaean cosmic system (First Man, Living Spirit, ordering of worlds).
- ¶5: Systematic eschatological mechanics (purification of light, dissolution, fire, Last Statue) in the same cosmological register; no institutional/pastoral turn.
- ¶6: Concise cosmological definition (Death = mixing with Darkness) wholly within the system; manuscript page marker removed per rules.
- ¶7: Eschatological periodization (new aeon/new earth; enthronement; unveiling) presented as cosmic process rather than exhortation.
- ¶8: Internal temporal correspondence (measure-to-measure) extending the systematic scheme; curly-brace artifact removed.
- ¶9: Cosmic unveiling and consummation imagery expressed as systematic eschatological mechanism (treasury, ambrosia/perfume as internal divine dispensation), without Gospel citation.
- ¶10: Degree/measure language (“same measure,” “each power in its aeon”) signals substrate-style ordered cosmology.
- ¶11: Impersonal description of post-temporal state (beyond measure/time; unveiling permanence) continuing the internal eschatological architecture.
- ¶12: Summative closure of the three-day scheme with “reckoning/number” typical of systematic classification teaching.
- ¶13: Speech-transition (“that I have proclaimed to you”) functions as discourse stitching rather than substrate exposition.
- ¶14: Cosmic mechanics of mixture and later separation-by-fire, plus Last Statue construction—fully internal system mapping; page marker removed.
- ¶15: Base content remains cosmological (loss of light, separation from living air/breath, relation to Last Statue), but the dropped ending uses moral-judicial rhetoric and the “blasphemed…Holy Spirit” motif suggestive of later Christianized polemic.
- ¶16: Formal definitional closure (temporal vs eternal) consistent with the chapter’s systematic twofold scheme; no institutional or citation markers.

## Chapter 40
### Concerning the Three Things that were established by the Light

*The chapter is overwhelmingly eschatological-cosmological teaching in an impersonal, systematic threefold structure (new earth/coffin/final lump and eternal separation). Aside from a brief hagiographic opening, there is no clear institutional or NT overlay; the “sinners/condemned by deeds” language reads as part of the cosmological mechanics rather than pastoral regulation.*

Three things would be fashioned by the Light through necessity. For there can be no other way nor other result, to allow them to stay without coming about. For the Darkness, the Enemy, must be bound by them; he who stood up against the Light from the beginning. Due to this, these things have no (other) result, to allow them to stay without coming about.

Now, the first thing is the new luminous earth, which was set and built on top of this earth [ ... ] it, namely that alien body [ ... ] built upon it.

The second thing is this: They will [ ... ] and they [ ... ] the new earth, the coffin [ ... ] of the dark fire, the companion of the Enemy.

The third thing is the souls of all the sinners who have been condemned by their deeds. They were [ ... ] over them woe; they have been condemned according to the wicked deeds they had committed, and due to this they will be crucified on the Enemy, he who had raised himself up against the Greatness.

They will seal up this final lump when all the likenesses and images of every shape will be nailed in it. Also, those will be bound by this last fetter for eternity, and be placed as a footstool and a mat and a rag for this coffin [ ... ] which is the prison of the Enemy. It will be placed, being a boundary-marker and a great void between the two, between the Darkness and the Enemy, so that they cannot reach one another, nor can they hear their voices; rather they will become sterile as they are separated from each other for ever and ever.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Formulaic hagiographic opening (“Once again… disciples… mystery of his knowledge”), functioning as editorial container rather than teaching.
- ¶2: Impersonal, systemic exposition: a numbered triad grounded in cosmological necessity, with personified principles (Light/Darkness/Enemy) and no audience-directed application.
- ¶3: Continues the numbered triad with cosmic architecture (new earth built over this earth); remains cosmos→cosmos despite lacunae.
- ¶4: Second item of the triad; technical eschatological mechanism (coffin/prison imagery) aimed at binding the Enemy, not institutional exhortation. (Page marker removed.)
- ¶5: Third item completes the triad and remains within eschatological mechanics (souls affixed to the Enemy). The moral vocabulary (“sinners/condemned”) reads here as part of the cosmic sorting process rather than later church discipline.
- ¶6: Highly characteristic core cosmological-eschatological description: ‘final lump’, eternal fetter, boundary/void, and sterilizing separation of Darkness from the Enemy—system mechanics without citations or institutional referents.

---

## Chapter 41
### Concerning the Three Blows that befell the Enemy because of the Light.

*The chapter is essentially a compact, systematic eschatological teaching: a threefold sequence of defeats/bindings of the Enemy by the Light. Only the opening dialogue-attribution (“Once again he turned, and says:”) functions as late compilation frame; the rest stays cosmos→cosmos (wars, binding, dissolution in fire, final separation and confinement) without institutional/pastoral application.*

The Darkness itself, the Enemy, has received three bad blows with three wars; and worse dangers from the Light in those three wars.

The first: (The Enemy) was brought out to the middle and separated from his dark earth, whence he had departed. He was vanquished in that first war, caught and [bound] by the Living Soul.

The second blow is the time when he will be dissolved and melted, and he perishes in this great fire. He is destroyed and overthrown, from the images that are all by which he exists. And he will be gathered in [to the] fetter corresponding to his archetype, and be made like he was from the first beginning.

The third blow that will befall the Enemy is the 'rolling back' [at the] end when everything will be separated, and male will be divided [from] female. Now, the male will be bound in the lump, but the female [will be thrown] in to the tomb. For it will be divided [ ... by a great] stone in their midst, for generation upon generation, for ever and ever.

This is the way that the binding of the Enemy will come about, in a fetter that is burdensome and strong, one from which there is never escape; because [they have achieved] his binding and bound him for ever. And they have achieved his being set apart, and he has been set apart for ever.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: The content is core-like (impersonal cosmological statement of a threefold structure), but the introductory turn-and-says formula is a classic redactional dialogue frame marker; stripping it yields a self-standing teaching incipit.
- ¶2: Systematic numbered sequence (“The first”) describing what happened in cosmic war and binding; all referents are internal to the Manichaean cosmological drama (Enemy, dark earth, Living Soul), with no application voice.
- ¶3: Eschatological process-description (dissolution, melting, perishing in great fire; regathering into a fetter ‘corresponding to his archetype’) remains within a closed cosmic mechanism—typical of the older expository substrate.
- ¶4: Still impersonal and structural: final separation (‘rolling back’), gendered division, lump/tomb confinement, great stone barrier. No institutional identification or exhortation; the imagery functions as cosmic eschatology, not social pointing.
- ¶5: Summative closure of the mechanism (binding in an inescapable fetter; permanent set-apartness) stays within the cosmic system and lacks second-person address; the repetitive finality reads as doctrinal exposition rather than pastoral enforcement.

## Chapter 42
### Concerning the Three Vessels

*Dominantly core cosmological-mechanical exposition (Living Spirit’s three garments managing discharge/sweeping of the three dark vessels) expressed through extended correspondential analogies (doctor/medicines; child in womb/garments). Later compilation frame appears in the opening and in speech-attribution seams (¶1–2, ¶13), and a clear late application/pastoral drift appears in the second-half advantages list where the teaching is applied to contemporary human conditions ("today"), ascetic practice (fasting), and institutional roles (apostles).*

Tell us, and instruct us about these three vessels that [ ... ] and were discharged from above, and how they were discharged and separated from all the rulers who are arranged in the heavens. For it is a necessity for us to know the manner in which the three vessels have been discharged, while the rulers were flourishing in their body.

Happen you know, that when these vessels were discharged, they were discharged in [ ... ] slaughter, but those [ ... ] these vessels from them.

They are like the form of a man who has fallen into a purulent sickness. Other wounds and diseases are in his body, and even bile and other poisons are in his inner limbs. He achieves good health by a wise doctor, who gives him one remedy to drink that causes his purulent sickness to break out above, and gives him another medicine to drink that causes his sickness to discharge below, and he gives him yet another medicine to drink that cures his wounds for him [ ... ] calmly and silently.

Through the skill of a wise doctor, with his effective medicines to consume that take away all sicknesses in this manner and dissolve all magic, the doctors shall [ ... ] they work cures through the aroma of the medicine, other wounds [ ... ] what is hidden, they at last tear them out from [ ... ] manifested above and below [ ... through] the aroma of edible medicines that were placed upon them.

This is also what these vessels of water, of darkness and of fire are like, which are hidden in the body of all the rulers above; while the Living Spirit is like the wise doctor. These three healing drugs are the three garments that the Living Spirit assumes upon his body: of wind, of water, and of fire. Through them he has set in order the things below. For by those three garments he has been displayed to the rulers who are above, with hard-heartedness, with shaking and fear, with derangement; from them he discharged the three vessels, those of water, of darkness and of fire.

The case of these vessels is just like a child who is in his mother's womb and is swathed by seven garments. The first is the marrow, the second is the bones, the third is the tendons, the fourth is the flesh, the fifth the veins, the sixth the blood, the seventh the skin of the body that covers him. The other garment also [ ... / ... in the womb of his mother. His mother also is just like] this child. The marrow and the bones, the sinews and flesh, the veins and blood and skin, together with the garment that she wears and the linen that swathes her, total nine. Together with the nine of the child, these and the others, they shall complete and make eighteen garments. Now, you shall find the soul of this child is entwined within all these.

And look see: When the smell of the action of the demon, which brings about the stench of birth, will reveal the child; immediately [ ... ] shall [ ... ] all [these things] and it emerges from a crack that has become visible, while no tear nor wound occurs in the body of the child. In the same way as this child opens up the womb of his mother, so is it also with these vessels of water, of darkness and of fire. The actions that open up his mother to the child, and she shakes him out and reveals him; these are the three living garments that the Living Spirit assumed on his body.

For by the aspect of the garment of water [ ... / ... ] from the rulers; he moved [ ... ] they are the poison and death with which their veins and entrails are filled.

By the aspect of the garment of wind, in which he was revealed [ ... ] he has swept away and erased the entire destructive shadow and dirt, discharged it down to the earth. Afterwards he swept it out and confined it in the vessel of darkness that surrounds the universe.

Also, by the garment of living fire, through its splendour and vigour, he discharged the fire of the darkness from all the rulers and threw it down upon this earth. He swept it another time from the earth and confined it in the vessel that surrounds all the worlds, and they call it the wall of great fire.

Now, those three garments, through which he established these three things, he did not peel them off all at once; but he stripped them off one by one at the appropriate time [ ... ] down to the earth [ ... ]

In this way the universe of the [ ... ... ] his vessels [ ... ] these vessels like [ ... ]

The use of the matter, and the management of these vessels that were swept out and discharged from above to earth, have brought an advantage and benefit of twelve great deeds. Afterwards, they were swept from the earth and pushed outside to the edges of the worlds and to the extremity.

The first deed is this: So that evil-doing does not increase in the universe, neither in the creations nor in the trees [ ... it has set i]tself up in three things.

The [first is / ...]

The second is error [ ... ... ] For, if it were so, if [the vessel of water] had not been swept down from above, but rather the waters existing up till now in the rulers above [ ... / ... the] torrents that would flow [ ... ] and the droplets that would [ ... ] down, so lust would have sprouted [ ... i]n the trees and in the fruits as well as all flesh [ ... ] down [ ... / ... ]

Not only this, but if the vessel of darkness had not been swept out and thrown down to the earth, nor swept from the earth and cast outside the region of the worlds into this pit; again the drops of darkness, which will drip down from it, and the torrents that will continue to flow down from above to the earth and assume the trees and all flesh, would have multiplied,

Furthermore, if the fire were in the rulers of the worlds of the heavens until today, and had not been swept out and cast down to the earth, and swept from the earth and shut up inside the vessel of fire that surrounds the worlds; [ ... ] this fire [ ... ... ] above through the torrent [ ... the torrent ... ] below;

and the dark fire would have multiplied in the tree and in fruit and in all flesh.

The second deed: Al[l] the heavens [ ... / ... ] of the sediment and the pollution [ ... ... ] so that [ ... / ... ] surpasses in them [ ... ]

[The third]: The heavens and the firmaments [ ... / ... b]urden [ ... ] relieved from the weight-carriers [ ... ] and it ceased being heavy. This rich one [ ... ... ] above.

The fourth: That they might purify and cleanse their dwelling places [ ... / ... ]

The fifth: Because the greatness and the richness that dwells in these ships, as it [ ... ] you might find them living in these ships, purified and holy.

The sixth: That you might find the mixed, incomplete, and unconfined temples above at rest and purified; since rest will reach them and pass through them and purify them.

The seventh: [ ... ] nature alone of the vessels, which will advance and extend to their places and sites [ ... / ... ] in [ ... ]

The eighth: That great enclosures and walls would be made, and confine everything.

The tenth deed: That they would become releasers and purifiers of everything at the end of the universe like the fire [ ... / ... ] burns [ ... ]

The eleventh deed: [ ... ] will make a crossing [ ... ] and the entire ruling-power [ ... ]

to the outer land, for they were not envious of this [ ... ] a man who is richer than them; because the aeons of light are outside [ ... ] the place [ ... ] of peace, the place of honour and grace, the place where the joy of the householders who dwell in it shall never pass away nor recede.

The twelfth: That you might find all the deeds of loss hidden from the aeons of light, because of the wall of fire that surrounds the entire building; and these unclean things are displayed (only) in its [ ... ]

There was a need for these twelve great deeds because the vessels that were discharged and swept out from the firmaments to the earth, and were also swept from the earth to the extr[emity) ... / ... ] the three words of life watch over them that [ ... / ... ar]e set over them in great power [ ... ] that they might watch over them at the last time; because of this, they are firmly set thus in these fetters,

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Substantive cosmological question (vessels discharged from rulers in the heavens) is teaching-structural; the narrative set-up ‘Once again… disciples… enlightener’ is compilation frame.
- ¶2: Core begins after the speech tag, giving the mechanism of discharge; the initial ‘enlightener…to his disciples’ is an editorial container.
- ¶3: Extended correspondential analogy (illness/poison ↔ hidden vessels; remedies ↔ purifying agents) stays internal to a cosmic-mechanical model; impersonal explanatory voice.
- ¶4: Continuation of the same technical analogy (process-language, hidden/manifested, above/below discharge). “magic” here functions inside the medical simile rather than as polemic against contemporary practitioners.
- ¶5: Clear core mapping cosmos→cosmos: vessels (dark elements) embedded in rulers; Living Spirit’s three light ‘garments’ as operative instruments; systematic triadic structure and mechanism of discharge.
- ¶6: Numbered anatomical layering (7/9/18 garments) is characteristic systematic correspondential teaching; second-person “you shall find” functions as explanatory rhetoric within the model, not institutional exhortation.
- ¶7: Correspondential birth-mechanics mapped directly to cosmic discharge-mechanics (garments as operative forces). No move toward contemporary institutions.
- ¶8: Cosmic physiology applied to rulers (veins/entrails filled with poison/death) and the ‘aspect’ of the water-garment as the operative trigger—internal system description.
- ¶9: Process sequence (reveal → sweep/discharge → sweep out → confine) is core cosmological mechanics; ‘vessel of darkness surrounding the universe’ is internal cosmic architecture.
- ¶10: Triadic garment-operation continues; ‘wall of great fire’ as cosmic boundary structure is core system vocabulary.
- ¶11: Timing/sequence regulation (one-by-one at the appropriate time) is core ‘how it works’ mechanics, not moral application.
- ¶12: Fragmentary but continues the impersonal cosmological explanation; no frame/pastoral markers.
- ¶13: Core introduces a structured twelvefold advantages list tied to cosmic management; the prefatory ‘he says to his disciples’ is editorial framing.
- ¶14: System-internal rationale (‘so that… in the universe… creations… trees’) for the cosmic management; still explanatory rather than hortatory.
- ¶15: Too lacunose to evaluate further; no overt late-layer markers.
- ¶16: Starts as core counterfactual cosmological mechanics (what would happen if the water-vessel remained in rulers), then pivots to editorial present (‘today’) and moral/institutional exempla (‘saint’).
- ¶17: Core mechanics (drips/torrents of darkness affecting trees and flesh) is extended into a ‘today’ comparison—clear application pivot.
- ¶18: Counterfactual cosmological containment logic continues; ‘until today’ may reflect later rhetorical anchoring, but the paragraph remains entirely within cosmic process and elements.
- ¶19: Begins with system-internal consequence (dark fire in vegetation/flesh) then shifts to community practice (‘fast’)—a clear late pastoral application.
- ¶20: Meta-discourse in direct address (“Behold, I have taught you…”) functions as editorial/compilation rhetoric rather than impersonal cosmological exposition; no extractable substrate segment.
- ¶21: Advantages list returns to cosmic purification of heavens/firmaments—system-internal benefit language.
- ¶22: Cosmic engineering/purification motif (‘weight-carriers’) is characteristic core cosmography.
- ¶23: Brief but remains in cosmic domain (dwelling places of heavenly structures).
- ¶24: ‘Ships’ here are cosmic vessels/vehicles; descriptive purification goal is internal to the aeonic system.
- ¶25: ‘Temples above’ functions as cosmic architecture (not contemporary cult-sites); purification/rest mechanics remain internal.
- ¶26: Internal spatial-ordering of vessels (‘places and sites’) continues the cosmic management rationale.
- ¶27: Boundary-making (enclosures/walls) is core cosmological containment logic.
- ¶28: Institutional/historical marker ‘apostles’ introduces church-role actors as agents in end-time sign-giving; this crosses from cosmic architecture to community/institutional narrative.
- ¶29: Eschatological purification described as cosmic function (‘releasers and purifiers… at the end of the universe’), using elemental fire as internal analogy.
- ¶30: Highly lacunose; remaining wording reads like cosmic-eschatological process rather than explicit institutional rule. Classification uncertain but no clear late markers survive.
- ¶31: Descriptive cosmography of the outer Land/Aeons of Light; impersonal beatitude-place formulae remain internal to the Light-world.
- ¶32: Containment-by-boundary (‘wall of fire’) and shielding the Aeons of Light is core architectural cosmology.
- ¶33: Mostly core: cosmic rationale, guardianship (‘three words of life’), fetters/bound containment; ends with a compiler-style first-person reminder indicating a later rhetorical layer.

## Chapter 43
### Concerning the Vessels.

*This chapter is overwhelmingly the technical cosmological teaching layer: a stepwise account of how the Father of Life/Living Spirit discharges, gathers, and confines the three dark vessels, leaving measurable residues in the cosmos. The only later material is the dialogue apparatus (“disciples…”, “apostle…”, “enlightener…”) and brief audience-address cues; there is no NT citation and no institutional/pastoral extension.*

When the Father of Life discharged these three vessels from above, they of water and of darkness and of fire, did he perhaps discharge them to their pits at the edge of the firmament? Or did he discharge them first to this earth, and then he swept them from the earth and threw them to their places?

when the Father of Life was about to discharge these three vessels he poured the three upon this earth, one by one at its (appointed) hour and season. He discharged them and cast them down to the earth.

Then he bailed and scooped them from this earth in these three garments, they of wind and of water and of fire, which swathed his body. He poured and made them flow into the three pits [ ... w]hich he had [es]tablished during the construc[tio]n of the worlds [ ... ] they [ ... ] something remained behind on the face of the earth from these three vessels,

The element that is left in water is the pungency and the salinity of this salty inner sea that surrounds the worlds.

And the remnant that is left from the darkness is this black mountain that exists in the surroundings of the sunrise. For when the sun would shine over the world no sunlight is visible there, because the darkness that gushes from it manifests above it.

And the remnant that remains behind from the fire, upon the earth, are all the mountains and islands from where fumes of fire gush up and are visible. For these things are the remnant of the great vessel, the vestige that was conjoined with this fire that comes up from the earth; together with the mountains and hills [ ... ] places where no fruit sprouts on the trees [ ... ] greater than that place from the limb and [ ... / ... ] the trees shall not bear fruit in that place.

the vessels that surround the universe, and why they were not discharged from above into the ditches prepared for them (straightaway); but were firstly poured upon the earth, and afterwards were swept from the earth and cast down into their pits?

When the Father of Life discharged these vessels from above upon the earth, he poured them out for this reason: so that also the water and the darkness and the fire that exist in these three earths would be drawn and ascend towards they that had come down from above, and the earths would be purified and cleansed. Due to this he poured these vessels down to the earth, of water and of darkness and of fire, so that also the water and darkness and fire that exist in the earths would be drawn up towards they that had descended from above.

Now, first of all, he discharged the water, the waste, the poison of the rulers who are above. He cleansed the firmaments of the [water ... ] he poured down the dark waters. [Just as the] waters fell from heaven to earth, so also the waters below in the earths were drawn and came up from below towards the waters that had come from above to the earths. The waters [were] purified of the dark poison that exists in them. Then he swept them outside and made them a great vessel and a wall of sea that surrounds the universe, and on which the ships sail.

After the water, he discharged the darkness. He purified the firmaments and dried from them the darkness with which they are weighed down. He discharged it down to the earth. Just as he had purified the firmaments, so also he purified the earths. The darkness that is in the earths was drawn up towards the darkness that had been discharged from above. He swept them both out and shut them in that [ ... ] of darkness [ ... / ... ] the water.

And he discharged the fire from heaven to earth, [ ... at] that time again the fire that was shut [in] the earth below was drawn and came up towards its kind. Fire was added to fire. Afterwards, he swept it out and cast it into the outer underworld, which [he had constructed for] them outside the universe.

In the same way that he purified the firmaments above as a place for the light-givers to journey [ ... / ... ] so also he purified the earths below and swept from them the fire and the darkness and the water [ ... ] He has purified them for the ascent of these three powers, they of the wind and the water and the fire, which ascend before the Porter and traverse the earths.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Substantive cosmological question (vessels, firmament, pits, earth) is teaching-structural; the Q&A attribution is later hagiographic/frame wrapping.
- ¶2: Core is impersonal process description (discharge in ordered times/seasons); “apostle” and direct instructional address mark the compilation frame.
- ¶3: The mechanics (garments, pits, construction of worlds, residue) stay within the cosmic system and read as old teaching; the closing “you can find…” is a later didactic gloss addressed to hearers.
- ¶4: Pure correspondential residue-teaching (dark remainder → sensory quality; sea surrounding worlds) with no frame or institutional reach.
- ¶5: Cosmos→cosmos description: residue of Darkness mapped to a cosmographic feature (black mountain) with an optical effect; no later citation or exhortation.
- ¶6: Technical cosmological etiology of volcanic/fire phenomena as residue of the vessel; entirely internal to the system (no institutional/polemical application).
- ¶7: Underlying content is a structural question about cosmological procedure (surrounding vessels; timing/sequence). The disciple-address and “our master” belong to the later instructional frame.
- ¶8: Core causal mechanism (like-to-like attraction; purification of the earths) is systematic cosmology; “enlightener… disciples” is compilation frame and “Happen you know” is didactic address.
- ¶9: Sequential technical exposition (first water; firmaments; attraction; purification; formation of encircling sea-vessel) with no frame/pastoral overlay; ships are part of the cosmic architecture here.
- ¶10: Second step in a systematic triad (water→darkness→fire): cosmos-internal mechanics of cleansing, drawing-up, and confinement.
- ¶11: Completes the triadic process with the same internal logic (kind-to-kind attraction; sweeping; outer underworld confinement).
- ¶12: Impersonal system-closure: purification prepares “light-givers” and the ascent of the three powers; “Porter” functions as a named cosmic office within the architecture, not as an institutional referent.

## Chapter 44
### Concerning the Sea Giant

*Aside from a brief hagiographic opener, this chapter is a continuous cosmological-mechanical exposition: how the three wheels are turned and washed, how the waste is deposited in the sea, how the sea giant is formed and sealed with astral impressions, and the threefold function of the giant within the cosmic architecture. No NT citation formulas, no institutional application, and the analogical coin/craftsman comparison remains internal to the system (cosmos→cosmos). The high chapter-level ‘fatigue’ signal is not borne out by clear pastoral grafting here; the main late element is the opening frame.*

After the Living Spirit constructed things and the worlds, he came up and set himself upon this earth. He gave a sign to the King of Glory, who turned the three wheels and caused the wheels that are found [ ... ] up above all the mountains; he spread forth the wind to them, he breathed on them, he stirred them up, he washed the earth with the waters. And the waste and the accretion of the three vessels, they of water and of darkness and of fire, which had been discharged upon the earth, he gathered them and deposited them in the sea that is within the walls and the vessels; because of this sea waters are salty. For they have received salt and bitterness from the washing out and cleansing of the three vessels.

When the Father of Life came, rising in his ascent to the heights, he discharged the wheel of the stars; he washed it out and loosened all the sediment that was in it. He cast it down to this great inner sea like [ ... ] of the mountain. And that waste, the sediment of the washing out of the wheel, he cast it down and mixed it in with the sea waters. Its own thought collected it together; and the thought gathered the waste together in this way. It made it fast and fixed it [ ... ]

The sea giant had stamped upon him the seal of the seconds and the hours, the seal of the days and months and years, the impression of the stars and the signs of the zodiac [ ... ] He came into existence from them and was discharged from their power and cast into the sea. The images and the seals and aspects and doctrines and counsels of [ ... ] were sealed upon the body of that giant, because he is the residue of them all. Consequently, each star that will shine, and each sign of the zodiac that will turn: of one he shall be its inducement, and of another its confirmation.

He was discharged into the sea like this, the way a cloud might discharge rainwater into the midst of the sea. They compelled him totally through the action of the power and the helmsmanship of the angels. He was swept down to the mill, the place where he was constructed in the setting up of the universe; and the sea also conceived him [ ... ] for its part [ ... ] it being his chief sculptor and chief craftsman. It sculpted him in a likeness, he being changed more than the other residue of the sea bodies.

This matter is comparable with a craftsman who might take the stamped impressions of many coins and cast them on the fire, and mould them [to a single] image. These thousands of impressions and thousands of shapes [are perfected] and make a single body [ ... ]

This is also the case with the sea giant, who too was moulded and sculpted by the power of the lust inside him from many doctrines and counsels that belong to the residue of the wheel of the sphere.

The archetype of the giant was constructed for three things.

The first: That the sphere would be relieved and purified of the burden present in it. In the same way that the ten firmaments were purified and relieved of the burden of the three vessels; so was it also done to the sphere, that it too might be relieved and purified of the burden and the waste inside it.

The second: That he might add a [fetter] and a prison to the wheel and the signs of the zodiac that are bound to it, so that when they wish to go up above, you can find the sea giant dragging them back as by an iron [fetter], and they are unable to go up; and when the powers of the wheel would take a decision on a guileful thing, and it enters their mind to come down to the earth, they are not able to descend because of this stake that is nailed to the wheel upon the [...] of the firmaments. This is one. However, this other one, because of its sleepless watchers that are set over it, it is turned all the time.

The third thing: That the wind and the water and the fire that gush from the circuit of the sea, and the rivers that flow upon the face of the earth discharging into the sea, and the rainwaters that come to ground, and the waste and rebirth that come down to it; that sea will be a hostel and gathering place for all of these.

As for all the power and the life that comes down to the sea, and in the breath that the sea giant will breathe and draw it into him that he might disturb the sea [ ... a] fullness and a [ ... ] becomes between his movement [ ... ] purify; but the sea [ ... ]

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Formulaic hagiographic/dialogue frame (‘Once again… speaks to his disciples’).
- ¶2: Process-mechanical cosmology (three wheels; washing/cleansing; deposition of residue in sea) with no appeal to authority or contemporary institutions; impersonal explanatory voice.
- ¶3: Cosmic engineering of the astral wheel and its sediment; ‘thought’ operates as an internal cosmological force, not moral exhortation.
- ¶4: Systematic astral-correspondential sealing (time units, stars, zodiac) onto a cosmic being; entirely cosmos→cosmos mapping.
- ¶5: Mythic-mechanical formation sequence (discharge, angelic ‘helmsmanship’, cosmic ‘mill’, sea as craftsman). No institutional or paraenetic turn.
- ¶6: Internal analogy explaining cosmic formation (many impressions → one body). No Gospel/NT citation formula; functions as correspondential exposition, not later moral application.
- ¶7: Continues the formation mechanism and ties it back to sphere-residue; ‘lust’ functions as an internal cosmological power here, not an exhortation.
- ¶8: Numbered structural transition (‘three things’) characteristic of systematic teaching layers.
- ¶9: Cosmic ‘purification’ described as a mechanical unloading of waste across firmaments/sphere; not ethical/pastoral purification language.
- ¶10: Cosmic restraining mechanism (fetter/prison/stake; watchers; wheel/zodiac motion control). Minor second-person phrasing (‘you can find’) remains descriptive, not an address to church practice.
- ¶11: Third functional purpose, framed as cosmic hydraulics/elemental circulation; ‘hostel and gathering place’ describes cosmic collection, not community institution.
- ¶12: Heavily lacunose, but remains within the same cosmological mechanism (life/power entering sea; giant’s breath; purification process). Dating is less secure due to damage, yet no markers of frame/overlay/pastoral are present.
